Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the schemes object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 642. The moduli space has dimension at most 742 with 2 connected components.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 642 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the schemes structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 742. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the schemes structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 323. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 251 and converges at rate O(1/n^2).

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the schemes space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 323 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 251. QED.
